upper bounds of Spearman’s footrule under General Missing Case.

We first prove the following result:

Lemma E.14
.

Suppose
X,Y\in\mathcal{V}^{n}
and for
0<m_{1},m_{2},m_{3}<n
such that
m_{1}+m_{2}+m_{3}<n
,
let
V=\{1,\ldots,m_{2}\}
,
U=\{m_{2}+1,\ldots,m_{2}+m_{1}\}
and
W=\{n-m_{3}+1,\ldots,n\}
be subsets of indices. Suppose

X(1)<\ldots<X(m_{2})
, and

Y(m_{2}+1)<\ldots<Y(m_{2}+m_{1})
.
Let
(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})})\in(\mathcal{X}(Y;U,[n]\setminus W,n),\mathcal{X}(X;V,[n]\setminus W,n))\cap\mathcal{Y}(W,n)

be imputations of
X,Y
for indices

U,V
such that

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in U}I\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i)<\min(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)=r_{1},

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in V}I\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(i)<\min(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)=r_{2},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in W}I\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i)<\min(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W}\right)=r_{3}.

Then, for any
i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U
, we have

\displaystyle R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}({i}),X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}\right)=r_{3}+r_{1}+R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\right).

(208)

For any
i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V
, we have

\displaystyle R\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}({i}),Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)=m_{3}-r_{3}+r_{2}+R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\right).

(209)

For any
r_{1}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{1}\}
,
r_{2}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{2}\}
,
r_{3}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{3}\}
, we have

\displaystyle R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}({i+m_{2}}),X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}\right)=p^{(r_{1})}_{i}+r_{3},\text{ for any }i\in\{1,\ldots,m_{1}\},

(210)

and

\displaystyle R\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}({i}),Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)=q^{(r_{2})}_{i}+m_{3}-r_{3},\text{ for any }i\in\{1,\ldots,m_{2}\},

(211)

where
p^{(r_{1})}_{i}=I\left(i\leq m_{1}-r_{1}\right)(n-m_{3}-i+1)+I\left(i>m_{1}-r_{1}\right)(m_{1}-i+1)
, and
q^{(r_{2})}_{i}=I\left(i\leq m_{2}-r_{2}\right)(n-m_{3}-i+1)+I\left(i>m_{2}-r_{2}\right)(m_{2}-i+1)
.

Proof.

First, we show (
208
) is true,
since
(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})})\in\mathcal{Y}(W,n)
,
then, according to the definition of
\mathcal{Y}(W,n)
,
we have
X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}\in\mathcal{Z}(W,[n],n)
.
Notice that, according to the definition of imputations,
we have
X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}
is an imputation of itself for indices
W
.
Since
\sum_{i\in W}I\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i)<\min(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W}\right)=r_{3}
,
then according to Lemma
E.2
,
for any
i\in[n]\setminus W
, we have

\displaystyle R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}({i}),X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}\right)=R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}({i}),(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W}\right)+r_{3}.

(212)

Further, since
X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}\in\mathcal{X}(Y;U,[n]\setminus W,n)
,
according to the definition of
\mathcal{X}(Y;U,[n]\setminus W,n)
, we also have

X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}\in\mathcal{Z}(U,[n]\setminus W,n)
.
According to the definition of
\mathcal{Z}(U,[n]\setminus W,n)
,
for any
i\in U
, we have

\displaystyle X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i)>\max(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W},\text{ or }X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i)<\min(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W}.

Hence, we also have
(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W}\in\mathcal{Z}(U,[n]\setminus W,n-m_{3})
.
Since
(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W}

is an imputation of itself for indices
U
, and we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in U}I\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i)<\min(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)=r_{1},

then according to Lemma
E.2
,
for any
i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}({i}),(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W}\right)\\
&=R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}({i}),(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}.\end{split}

(213)

Since
X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}
is an imputation of
X
for indices
U\cup W
,
for any
i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U
, we have

\displaystyle R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}({i}),(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)=R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right).

(214)

Combining (
212
),
(
213
),
and
(
214
), for any
i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U
, we have

\displaystyle R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}({i}),X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}\right)=r_{3}+r_{1}+R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\right).

This proves (
208
).

Similarly, we can show (
209
) is true.
Since
(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})})\in\mathcal{Y}(W,n)
,
then according to Lemma
E.9
,
we have
Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\in\mathcal{Z}(W,[n],n)
,
and

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in W}I\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(i)<\min(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W}\right)=m_{3}-r_{3}.

Notice that, according to the definition of imputations,

Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}
is an imputation of itself for indices
W
.
Hence, according to Lemma
E.2
,
for any
i\in[n]\setminus W
, we have

\displaystyle R\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}({i}),Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)=R\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}({i}),(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W}\right)+m_{3}-r_{3}.

(215)

Further, since
Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\in\mathcal{X}(X;V,[n]\setminus W,n)
, we have

Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\in\mathcal{Z}(V,[n]\setminus W,n)
.
Then according to the definition of
\mathcal{Z}(V,[n]\setminus W,n)
,
we have

\displaystyle Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(i)>\max(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W},\text{ or }Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(i)<\min(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W},\text{ for any }i\in V.

Hence, we also have
(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W}\in\mathcal{Z}(V,[n]\setminus W,n-m_{3})
.
Since
(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W}

is an imputation of itself for indices
V
, and we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in V}I\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(i)<\min(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)=r_{2}.

then according to Lemma
E.2
,
for any
i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&R\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}({i}),(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W}\right)\\
&=R\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}({i}),(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)+r_{2}.\end{split}

(216)

Since
Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}
is an imputation of
Y
for indices
V\cup W
,
for any
i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V
, we have

\displaystyle R\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}({i}),(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)=R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right).

(217)

Combining (
215
),
(
216
),
and
(
217
), for any
i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V
, we have

\displaystyle R\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}({i}),Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)=m_{3}-r_{3}+r_{2}+R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\right).

This proves (
209
)
is true.

Next, we show (
210
) is true,
since
X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}\in\mathcal{X}(Y;U,[n]\setminus W,n)
,
then we have

\displaystyle X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i)>\max(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W},\text{ or }X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i)<\min(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W},\text{ for any }i\in U,

\displaystyle\text{and }X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i)>X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(j),\text{ if }Y(i)<Y(j),\text{ for any }i,j\in U.

Hence, we have
(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W}\in\mathcal{X}((Y(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W};U,[n]\setminus W,n-m_{3})
. Since

\sum_{i\in U}I\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i)<\min(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)=r_{1},

then according to Lemma
E.1
, for any
i\in\{1,\ldots,m_{1}\}
, we have

\displaystyle R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(m_{2}+i),(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W}\right)=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}n-m_{3}-i+1,&\text{ if }r_{1}=0,\\
p^{(r_{1})}_{i},&\text{ if }m_{1}>r>0,\\
m_{1}-i+1,&\text{ if }r_{1}=m_{2},\end{array}\right.

where
p^{(r_{1})}_{i}=I\left(m_{2}+i\leq m_{2}+m_{1}-r_{1}\right)(n-m_{3}-i+1)+I\left(m_{2}+i>m_{2}+m_{1}-r_{1}\right)(m_{1}-i+1)=I\left(i\leq m_{1}-r_{1}\right)(n-m_{3}-i+1)+I\left(i>m_{1}-r_{1}\right)(m_{1}-i+1)
.

Notice that
R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i),(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W}\right)=p^{(r_{1})}_{i}

is still true when
r_{1}=0
, or
r_{1}=m_{2}
. Combining this result with (
212
),
we have

\displaystyle R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}({i}),X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}\right)=p^{(r_{1})}_{i}+r_{3},\text{ for any }i\in U.

This completes our proof for (
210
).

Next, we show (
210
) is true,
since
Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\in\mathcal{X}(X;V,[n]\setminus W,n)
,
then we have

\displaystyle Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(i)>\max(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W},\text{ or }Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(i)<\min(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W},\text{ for any }i\in V,

\displaystyle\text{and }Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(i)>Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(j),\text{ if }X(i)<X(j),\text{ for any }i,j\in V.

Hence, we have
(Y_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W}\in\mathcal{X}((X(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W};V,[n]\setminus W,n-m_{3})
.
Since

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in V}I\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(i)<\min(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)=r_{2},

then according to Lemma
E.1
, for any
i\in\{1,\ldots,m_{2}\}
, we have

\displaystyle R\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(i),(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W}\right)

\displaystyle=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}n-m_{3}-i+1,&\text{ if }r_{2}=0,\\
I\left(i\leq m_{2}-r_{2}\right)(n-m_{3}-i+1)+I\left(i>m_{2}-r_{2}\right)(m_{2}-i+1),&\text{ if }m_{2}>r_{2}>0,\\
m_{2}-i+1,&\text{ if }r_{2}=m_{2}\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle=I\left(i\leq m_{2}-r_{2}\right)(n-m_{3}-i+1)+I\left(i>m_{2}-r_{2}\right)(m_{2}-i+1)

\displaystyle=p^{(r_{2})}_{i}.

Combining this result with (
215
),
we have

\displaystyle R\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}({i}),Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)=p^{(r_{2})}_{i}+m_{3}-r_{3}.

This proves (
211
) is true,
and completes our proof.
∎

Using the results of Lemma
E.14
,
we can show the following result:

Proposition E.15
.

Suppose
X,Y\in\mathcal{V}^{n}
and for
0<m_{1},m_{2},m_{3}<n
such that
m_{1}+m_{2}+m_{3}<n
,
let
V=\{1,\ldots,m_{2}\}
,
U=\{m_{2}+1,\ldots,m_{2}+m_{1}\}
and
W=\{n-m_{3}+1,\ldots,n\}
be subsets of indices. Suppose

X(1)<\ldots<X(m_{2})
, and

Y(m_{2}+1)<\ldots<Y(m_{2}+m_{1})
.
Let
(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})})\in(\mathcal{X}(Y;U,[n]\setminus W,n),\mathcal{X}(X;V,[n]\setminus W,n))\cap\mathcal{Y}(W,n)

be imputations of
X,Y
for indices

U\cup W,V\cup W
such that

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in U}I\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i)<\min(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)=r_{1},

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in V}I\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(i)<\min(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)=r_{2},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in W}I\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i)<\min(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W}\right)=r_{3}.

Denote
d_{i}=R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)
for any
i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)
. Then for any

r_{1}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{1}\}
,
r_{2}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{2}\}
, and
r_{2}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{3}\}
, we have

\displaystyle D\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}-q^{(r_{2})}_{i}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\in U}\left|p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|r_{1}-d_{i}-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|+g^{(r_{3})},

where
p^{(r_{1})}_{i}=I\left(i\leq m_{1}-r_{1}\right)(n-m_{3}-i+1)+I\left(i>m_{1}-r_{1}\right)(m_{1}-i+1)
,
q^{(r_{2})}_{i}=I\left(i\leq m_{2}-r_{2}\right)(n-m_{3}-i+1)+I\left(i>m_{2}-r_{2}\right)(m_{2}-i+1)
,
and

\displaystyle g^{(r_{3})}=\sum_{i=1}^{m_{3}}\left(I\left(i\leq m_{3}-r_{3}\right)|n+1-2i|\right)+\sum_{i=1}^{m_{3}}\left(I\left(i>m_{3}-r_{3}\right)|2m_{3}-2i+1-n|\right).

Proof.

To start, according to Lemma
E.14
, we have

\displaystyle R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}({i}),X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}\right)=r_{3}+r_{1}+R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\right),\text{ for any }i\in V,

and

\displaystyle R\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}({i}),Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)=q^{(r_{2})}_{i}+m_{3}-r_{3},\text{ for any }i\in\{1,\ldots,m_{2}\}.

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i),X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}\right)-R\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(i),Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)\right|\\
&=\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}-q^{(r_{2})}_{i}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|.\end{split}

(218)

Next, according to Lemma
E.14
, we also have

\displaystyle R\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}({i}),Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)=m_{3}-r_{3}+r_{2}+R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\right),\text{ for any }i\in U,

and

\displaystyle R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}({i+m_{2}}),X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}\right)=p^{(r_{1})}_{i}+r_{3},\text{ for any }i\in\{1,\ldots,m_{1}\}.

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}({i}),X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}\right)=p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}+r_{3},\text{ for any }i\in\{m_{2}+1,\ldots,m_{2}+m_{1}\}.

Hence,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sum_{i\in U}\left|R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i),X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}\right)-R\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(i),Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)\right|\\
&=\sum_{i\in U}\left|p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}+2r_{3}-m_{3}-r_{2}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\right)\right|.\end{split}

(219)

Next, according to Lemma
E.14
, for any
i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)
, we have

\displaystyle R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}({i}),X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}\right)=r_{3}+r_{1}+R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\right),

and

\displaystyle R\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}({i}),Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)=m_{3}-r_{3}+r_{2}+R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\right).

Hence,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i),X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}\right)-R\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(i),Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)\right|\\
&=\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|2r_{3}+r_{1}-m_{3}-r_{2}-d_{i}\right|.\end{split}

(220)

Now, since

(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})})\in\mathcal{Y}(W,n)

and

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in W}I\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i)<\min(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W}\right)=r_{3},

then according to Lemma
E.9
,
we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in W}\left|R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i),X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}\right)-R\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(i),Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)\right|=g^{(r_{3})}.

(221)

Finally, combining (
218
),
(
219
), (
220
)
and (
221
), we have

\displaystyle D\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i),X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}\right)-R\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(i),Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i),X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}\right)-R\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(i),Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\in U}\left|R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i),X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}\right)-R\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(i),Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i),X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}\right)-R\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(i),Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\in W}\left|R\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i),X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}\right)-R\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(i),Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}-q^{(r_{2})}_{i}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\in U}\left|p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|r_{1}-d_{i}-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|+g^{(r_{3})}.

This completes our proof.
∎

Finally, we show the following result:

Proposition E.16
.

Suppose
X,Y\in\mathcal{V}^{n}
and for
0<m_{1},m_{2},m_{3}<n
such that
m_{1}+m_{2}+m_{3}<n
,
let
V=\{1,\ldots,m_{2}\}
,
U=\{m_{2}+1,\ldots,m_{2}+m_{1}\}
and
W=\{n-m_{3}+1,\ldots,n\}
be subsets of indices. Suppose

X(1)<\ldots<X(m_{2})
, and

Y(m_{2}+1)<\ldots<Y(m_{2}+m_{1})
.
Let
(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})})\in(\mathcal{X}(Y;U,[n]\setminus W,n),\mathcal{X}(X;V,[n]\setminus W,n))\cap\mathcal{Y}(W,n)

be imputations of
X,Y
for indices

U\cup W
, and
V\cup W
, respectively, such that

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in U}I\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i)<\min(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)=r_{1},

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in V}I\left(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(i)<\min(Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)=r_{2},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in W}I\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(i)<\min(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})}(l))_{l\in[n]\setminus W}\right)=r_{3}.

For any

r_{1}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{1}\}
,
r_{2}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{2}\}
, and
r_{3}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{3}\}
,
denote

\displaystyle S^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}=\sum_{i\in V}I\left(R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}-q^{(r_{2})}_{i}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\geq 0\right),

and

\displaystyle R^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}=\sum_{i\in U}I\left(p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}-1+2r_{3}-m_{3}\geq 0\right),

where
p^{(r_{1})}_{i}=I\left(i\leq m_{1}-r_{1}\right)(n-m_{3}-i+1)+I\left(i>m_{1}-r_{1}\right)(m_{1}-i+1)
,
q^{(r_{2})}_{i}=I\left(i\leq m_{2}-r_{2}\right)(n-m_{3}-i+1)+I\left(i>m_{2}-r_{2}\right)(m_{2}-i+1)
.
For any
i\in\{-m_{1}-1-2m_{3},\ldots,m_{2}\}
,
let
s_{i}=\sum_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}I\left(d_{i}\leq i\right)
,
and denote
n^{\prime}=n-m_{1}-m_{2}-m_{3}
.
Then, for any
r_{1}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{1}-1\}
,
r_{2}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{2}\}
, and
r_{3}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{3}\}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}D(X_{*}^{(r_{1}+1,r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})})&=D(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})})\\
&+2S^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}-2s_{m_{3}+r_{2}-r_{1}-2r_{3}-1}+C_{1}^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})},\end{split}

(222)

for any
r_{1}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{1}\}
,
r_{2}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{2}-1\}
, and
r_{3}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{3}\}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}D(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2}+1,r_{3})})&=D(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})})\\
&-2R^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}+2s_{m_{3}+r_{2}-r_{1}-2r_{3}}+C_{2}^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}.\end{split}

(223)

and for any
r_{1}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{1}\}
,
r_{2}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{2}\}
, and
r_{3}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{3}-1\}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&D\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3}+1)},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3}+1)}\right)=D\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)+|2r_{3}+1-n|\\
&-|n+1-2(m_{3}-r_{3})|+2S^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}+2S^{(r_{1}+1,r_{2},r_{3})}-2m_{2}\\
&+2R^{(r_{1},r_{2}-1,r_{3})}+2R^{(r_{1},r_{2}-2,r_{3})}-2m_{1}\\
&+2s_{2r_{3}+r_{1}-r_{2}-m_{3}}+2s_{2r_{3}+r_{1}-r_{2}-m_{3}+1}-2n^{\prime}.\end{split}

(224)

where for any
r_{1}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{1}\}
,
r_{2}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{2}\}
, and
r_{3}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{3}\}
,

\displaystyle C_{1}^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}=-m_{2}+n^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\left|r_{1}+1-R\left(Y(m_{1}+m_{2}-r_{1}),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle-\left|n-m_{3}-m_{1}+r_{1}+1-R\left(Y(m_{1}+m_{2}-r_{1}),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|,

and

\displaystyle C_{2}^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}=m_{1}-n^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\left|r_{2}+1-R\left(X(m_{2}-r),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)-r_{1}-2r_{3}+m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle-\left|n-m_{3}-m_{2}+r_{2}+1-R\left(X(m_{2}-r),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)-r_{1}-2r_{3}+m_{3}\right|.

Proof.

First, we prove (
222
) is true.
According to Proposition
E.15
,
we have

\displaystyle D\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}-q^{(r_{2})}_{i}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\in U}\left|p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|r_{1}-d_{i}-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|+g^{(r_{3})},

and

\displaystyle D\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1}+1,r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}+1-q^{(r_{2})}_{i}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\in U}\left|p^{(r_{1}+1)}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|r_{1}+1-d_{i}-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|+g^{(r_{3})},

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&D\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1}+1,r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)-D\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)\\
&=\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}+1-q^{(r_{2})}_{i}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&-\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}-q^{(r_{2})}_{i}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&+\sum_{i\in U}\left|p^{(r_{1}+1)}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&-\sum_{i\in U}\left|p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&+\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|r_{1}+1-d_{i}-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&-\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|r_{1}-d_{i}-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|.\end{split}

(225)

Notice that, according to Lemma
E.4
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}+1-q^{(r_{2})}_{i}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&-\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}-q^{(r_{2})}_{i}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&=2\sum_{i\in V}I\left(R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}-q^{(r_{2})}_{i}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\geq 0\right)-m_{2},\\
&=2S^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}-m_{2},\end{split}

(226)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|d_{i}+r_{1}+1-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|-\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|d_{i}+r_{1}-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&=2\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}I\left(d_{i}+r_{1}-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\geq 0\right)-(n-m_{1}-m_{2}-m_{3})\\
&=2\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}I\left(d_{i}\geq m_{3}+r_{2}-r_{1}-2r_{3}\right)-n^{\prime}\\
&=2n^{\prime}-2\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}I\left(d_{i}<m_{3}+r_{2}-r_{1}-2r_{3}\right)-n^{\prime}.\end{split}

Since
m_{3},r_{1},r_{2},r_{3}
are integers, and for any
i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)
,

d_{i}
is an integer, we have

\displaystyle I\left(d_{i}<m_{3}+r_{2}-r_{1}-2r_{3}\right)=I\left(d_{i}\leq m_{3}+r_{2}-r_{1}-2r_{3}-1\right).

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|d_{i}+r_{1}+1-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&-\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|d_{i}+r_{1}-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&=n^{\prime}-2\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}I\left(d_{i}\leq m_{3}+r_{2}-r_{1}-2r_{3}-1\right)\\
&=n^{\prime}-2s_{m_{3}+r_{2}-r_{1}-2r_{3}-1}.\end{split}

(227)

Next, notice that

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in U}\left|p^{(r_{1}+1)}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle-\sum_{i\in U}\left|p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=m_{2}+1}^{m_{2}+m_{1}}\left|p^{(r_{1}+1)}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\sum_{i=m_{2}+1}^{m_{2}+m_{1}}\left|p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{m_{1}}\left|p^{(r_{1}+1)}_{i}-R\left(Y(m_{2}+i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{m_{1}}\left|p^{(r_{1})}_{i}-R\left(Y(m_{2}+i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|,

where
p^{(r_{1})}_{i}=I\left(i\leq m_{1}-r_{1}\right)(n-m_{3}-i+1)+I\left(i>m_{1}-r_{1}\right)(m_{1}-i+1)

for any
i\in\{1,\ldots,m_{1}\}
and
r_{1}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{1}\}
. Then, according to Lemma
E.5
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{m_{1}}\left|p^{(r_{1}+1)}_{i}-R\left(Y(m_{2}+i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{m_{1}}\left|p^{(r_{1})}_{i}-R\left(Y(m_{2}+i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|r_{1}+1-R\left(Y(m_{1}+m_{2}-r_{1}),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle-\left|n-m_{3}-m_{1}+r_{1}+1-R\left(Y(m_{1}+m_{2}-r_{1}),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle=C_{1}^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}+m_{2}-n^{\prime}.

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sum_{i\in U}\left|p^{(r_{1}+1)}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&-\sum_{i\in U}\left|p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&=C_{1}^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}+m_{2}-n^{\prime}.\end{split}

(228)

Put (
226
), (
227
),
and (
228
) back into (
225
),
we obtain

\displaystyle D(X_{*}^{(r_{1}+1,r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})})-D(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})})

\displaystyle=2S^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}-m_{2}+n^{\prime}-2t_{m_{3}+r_{2}-r_{1}-2r_{3}-1}+C_{1}^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}+m_{2}-n^{\prime}

\displaystyle=2S^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}-2s_{m_{3}+r_{2}-r_{1}-2r_{3}-1}+C_{1}^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}.

This proves (
222
) is true.

Similarly, we can show (
223
) is true.
According to Proposition
E.15
,
we have

\displaystyle D\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}-q^{(r_{2})}_{i}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\in U}\left|p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|r_{1}-d_{i}-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|+q^{(r_{3})},

and

\displaystyle D\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2}+1,r_{3})}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}-q^{(r_{2}+1)}_{i}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\in U}\left|p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}-1+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|r_{1}-d_{i}-r_{2}-1+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|+q^{(r_{3})},

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&D\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2}+1,r_{3})}\right)-D\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)\\
&=\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}-q^{(r_{2}+1)}_{i}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&-\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}-q^{(r_{2})}_{i}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&+\sum_{i\in U}\left|p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}-1+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&-\sum_{i\in U}\left|p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&+\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|r_{1}-d_{i}-r_{2}-1+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&-\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|r_{1}-d_{i}-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|.\end{split}

(229)

Notice that, according to Lemma
E.4
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sum_{i\in U}\left|p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}-1+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&-\sum_{i\in U}\left|p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&=-2\sum_{i\in U}I\left(p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}-1+2r_{3}-m_{3}\geq 0\right)+m_{1},\\
&=-2R^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}+m_{1},\end{split}

(230)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|d_{i}+r_{1}-r_{2}-1+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|-\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|d_{i}+r_{1}-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&=-2\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}I\left(d_{i}+r_{1}-r_{2}-1+2r_{3}-m_{3}\geq 0\right)+(n-m_{1}-m_{2}-m_{3})\\
&=-2\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}I\left(d_{i}\geq m_{3}+r_{2}-r_{1}-2r_{3}+1\right)+n^{\prime}\\
&=-2\left(n^{\prime}-\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}I\left(d_{i}<m_{3}+r_{2}-r_{1}-2r_{3}+1\right)\right)+n^{\prime}.\end{split}

Since
m_{3},r_{1},r_{2},r_{3}
are integers, and for any
i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)
,

d_{i}
is an integer, we have

\displaystyle I\left(d_{i}<m_{3}+r_{2}-r_{1}-2r_{3}+1\right)=I\left(d_{i}\leq m_{3}+r_{2}-r_{1}-2r_{3}\right).

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|d_{i}+r_{1}-r_{2}-1+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&-\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|d_{i}+r_{1}-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&=2s_{m_{3}+r_{2}-r_{1}-2r_{3}}-n^{\prime}.\end{split}

(231)

Next, notice that

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}-q^{(r_{2}+1)}_{i}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle-\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}-q^{(r_{2})}_{i}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in V}\left|q^{(r_{2}+1)}_{i}-R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)-r_{1}-2r_{3}+m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle-\sum_{i\in V}\left|q^{(r_{2})}_{i}-R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)-r_{1}-2r_{3}+m_{3}\right|.

Since
q^{(r_{2})}_{i}=I\left(i\leq m_{2}-r_{2}\right)(n-m_{3}-i+1)+I\left(i>m_{2}-r_{2}\right)(m_{2}-i+1)
, then according to Lemma
E.5
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in V}\left|q^{(r_{2}+1)}_{i}-R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)-r_{1}-2r_{3}+m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle-\sum_{i\in V}\left|q^{(r_{2})}_{i}-R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)-r_{1}-2r_{3}+m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|r_{2}+1-R\left(X(m_{2}-r),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)-r_{1}-2r_{3}+m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle-\left|n-m_{3}-m_{2}+r_{2}+1-R\left(X(m_{2}-r),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)-r_{1}-2r_{3}+m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle=C_{2}^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}-m_{1}+n^{\prime}.

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}-q^{(r_{2}+1)}_{i}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&-\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}-q^{(r_{2})}_{i}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&=C_{2}^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}-m_{1}+n^{\prime}.\end{split}

(232)

Put (
230
), (
231
),
and (
232
) back into (
229
),
we obtain

\displaystyle D(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2}+1,r_{3})})-D(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})})

\displaystyle=-2R^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}+m_{1}+2t_{m_{3}+r_{2}-r_{1}-2r_{3}}-n^{\prime}+C_{2}^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}-m_{1}+n^{\prime}

\displaystyle=-2R^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}+2s_{m_{3}+r_{2}-r_{1}-2r_{3}}+C_{2}^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}.

This proves (

) is true.

Finally, we show (
224
) is true. According to Proposition
E.15
,
we have

\displaystyle D\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}-q^{(r_{2})}_{i}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\in U}\left|p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|r_{1}-d_{i}-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|+g^{(r_{3})},

and

\displaystyle D\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3}+1)},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3}+1)}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}-q^{(r_{2})}_{i}+2r_{3}+2-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\in U}\left|p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}+2-m_{3}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|r_{1}-d_{i}-r_{2}+2r_{3}+2-m_{3}\right|+g^{(r_{3}+1)},

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&D\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3}+1)},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3}+1)}\right)-D\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)\\
&=\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}-q^{(r_{2})}_{i}+2r_{3}+2-m_{3}\right|\\
&-\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}-q^{(r_{2})}_{i}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&+\sum_{i\in U}\left|p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}+2-m_{3}\right|\\
&-\sum_{i\in U}\left|p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&+\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|r_{1}-d_{i}-r_{2}+2r_{3}+2-m_{3}\right|\\
&-\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|r_{1}-d_{i}-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|+g^{(r_{3}+1)}-g^{(r_{3})}.\end{split}

(233)

According to Lemma
E.11
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}g^{(r_{3}+1)}-g^{(r_{3})}=|2r_{3}+1-n|-|n+1-2(m_{3}-r_{3})|.\end{split}

(234)

According to Lemma
E.12
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}-q^{(r_{2})}_{i}+2r_{3}+2-m_{3}\right|\\
&-\sum_{i\in V}\left|R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)+r_{1}-q^{(r_{2})}_{i}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&=2\sum_{i\in V}I\left(r_{1}+2r_{3}+R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\right)-q^{(r_{2})}_{i}-m_{3}\geq 0\right)\\
&+2\sum_{i\in V}I\left(r_{1}+2r_{3}+R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\right)-q^{(r_{2})}_{i}-m_{3}+1\geq 0\right)-2m_{2}\\
&=2S^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}+2S^{(r_{1}+1,r_{2},r_{3})}-2m_{2}.\end{split}

(235)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sum_{i\in U}\left|p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}+2-m_{3}\right|\\
&-\sum_{i\in U}\left|p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&=2\sum_{i\in U}I\left(p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}+2r_{3}-m_{3}-r_{2}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\right)\geq 0\right)\\
&+2\sum_{i\in U}I\left(p^{(r_{1})}_{i-m_{2}}+2r_{3}-m_{3}-r_{2}-R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\right)+1\geq 0\right)-2m_{1}\\
&=2R^{(r_{1},r_{2}-1,r_{3})}+2R^{(r_{1},r_{2}-2,r_{3})}-2m_{1},\end{split}

(236)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|r_{1}-d_{i}-r_{2}+2r_{3}+2-m_{3}\right|\\
&-\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}\left|r_{1}-d_{i}-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\right|\\
&=2\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(V\cup U)}I\left(r_{1}-d_{i}-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}\geq 0\right)\\
&+2\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(V\cup U)}I\left(r_{1}-d_{i}-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}+1\geq 0\right)-2(n-m_{1}-m_{2}-m_{3})\\
&=2\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(V\cup U)}I\left(d_{i}\leq 2r_{3}+r_{1}-r_{2}-m_{3}\right)\\
&+2\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(V\cup U)}I\left(d_{i}\leq 2r_{3}+r_{1}-r_{2}-m_{3}+1\right)-2n^{\prime}\\
&=2s_{2r_{3}+r_{1}-r_{2}-m_{3}}+2s_{2r_{3}+r_{1}-r_{2}-m_{3}+1}-2n^{\prime}.\end{split}

(237)

Put (
234
), (
235
),
(
236
) and (
237
)
back into (
233
), we obtain

\displaystyle D\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3}+1)},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3}+1)}\right)-D\left(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})}\right)

\displaystyle=|2r_{3}+1-n|-|n+1-2(m_{3}-r_{3})|+2S^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}+2S^{(r_{1}+1,r_{2},r_{3})}-2m_{2}

\displaystyle+2R^{(r_{1},r_{2}-1,r_{3})}+2R^{(r_{1},r_{2}-2,r_{3})}-2m_{1}+2s_{2r_{3}+r_{1}-r_{2}-m_{3}}+2s_{2r_{3}+r_{1}-r_{2}-m_{3}+1}-2n^{\prime}.

This proves (

), and completes our proof.
∎

Algorithm 8

An efficient algorithm for computing exact upper bounds of Spearman’s Footrule under General Missing Case.

1:

X,Y\in\mathcal{V}^{n}
, where
X
and
Y
might be
partially observed.

2:

Maximum possible Spearman’s footrule distance between
X
and
Y
.

3:

Denote
m_{1}
as the number of pairs
(X(i),Y(i))
such that
Y(i)
is observed while

X(i)
is missing. Denote
m_{2}
as the number of pairs
(X(i),Y(i))
such that
X(i)
is observed while
Y(i)
is missing.
Denote
m_{3}
as the number of pairs such that
both
(X(i),Y(i))

are missing.

4:

If
m_{1}+m_{2}=0
, then run Algorithm
7
. However, if
m_{3}=0
, then run Algorithm
6
. If
m_{1}+m_{2}+m_{3}=n
, then return
\sum_{i=1}^{n}|i-(n-i+1)|
.

5:

Rank all observed data in
X
and
Y
.
Relabel
X
and
Y
such that

Y(1),\ldots,Y(m_{2})
are missing,
while
X(1),\ldots,X(m_{2})
are observed,
and
X(1)<\ldots,X(m_{2})
.
Let
X(m_{2}+1),\ldots,X(m_{2}+m_{2})

be missing, while
Y(m_{2}+1),\ldots,Y(m_{2}+m_{1})

are observed and
Y(m_{2}+1)<\ldots<Y(m_{2}+m_{1})
.
Let
(X(n-m_{3}+1),Y(n-m_{3}+1)),\ldots,(X(n),Y(n))
be missing.

6:

Denote
V=\{1,\ldots,m_{2}\}
,
U=\{m_{2}+1,\ldots,m_{2}+m_{1}\}
, and
W=\{n-m_{3}+1,\ldots,n\}
.

7:

For
i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)
, let

d_{i}=R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)
.

8:

For any
i\in\{1,\ldots,m_{2}\}
and
r_{2}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{2}\}
, let
q^{(r_{2})}_{i}=I\left(i\leq m_{2}-r_{2}\right)(n-m_{3}-i+1)+I\left(i>m_{2}-r_{2}\right)(m_{2}-i+1)
.

9:

For any
i\in\{1,\ldots,m_{1}\}
, let
p^{(0)}_{i}=I\left(i\leq m_{1}\right)(n-m_{3}-i+1)+I\left(i>m_{1}\right)(m_{1}-i+1)
.

10:

Run Algorithm
4
computing
s_{i}=\sum_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}I\left(d_{l}\leq i\right)
for any
i\in\{-m_{1}-m_{2}-m_{3}-1,\ldots,m_{1}+m_{2}+m_{3}+1\}
.

11:

Run Algorithm
4
computing

\displaystyle R^{(k)}=\sum_{i\in U}I\left(R\left(Y(i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)-p^{(0)}_{i-m_{2}}\leq k\right),

for any
k\in\{-m_{2}-m_{3}-1,\ldots,m_{3}-1\}
.

12:

For any
r_{2}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{2}\},r_{3}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{3}\}
, let

\displaystyle R^{(0,r_{2},r_{3})}=R^{(k)},\text{ if }-r_{2}-1+2r_{3}-m_{3}=k.

13:

for

r_{2}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{2}\}

do

14:

Run Algorithm
4
computing

\displaystyle S^{(k)}=\sum_{i\in V}I\left(q^{(r_{2})}_{i}-R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)\leq k\right),

for any
k\in\{-m_{3},\ldots,m_{1}+m_{3}\}
.

15:

For any
r_{1}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{1}\},r_{3}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{3}\}
, let

\displaystyle S^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}=S^{(k)},\text{ if }r_{1}+2r_{3}-m_{3}=k.

16:

end

for

17:

Initialize

\displaystyle D_{0,0,0}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{m_{2}}\left|n+i-1-R\left(X(i),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{m_{1}}\left|n-m_{3}+i-1-R\left(Y(m_{2}+i),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{m_{3}}|n+1-2i|+\sum_{i\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus(U\cup V)}|d_{i}+m_{3}|.

18:

for

r_{3}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{3}\}

do

19:

for

r_{2}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{2}\}

do

20:

for

r_{1}\in\{0,\ldots,m_{1}\}

do

21:

Denote
c_{1}=r_{1}+1-r_{2}+2r_{3}-m_{3}
, and compute

\displaystyle C_{1}^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}=-m_{2}+n^{\prime}+\left|c_{1}-R\left(Y(m_{1}+m_{2}-r_{1}),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)\right|

\displaystyle-\left|c_{1}+n-m_{3}-m_{1}-R\left(Y(m_{1}+m_{2}-r_{1}),(Y(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus V}\right)\right|.

22:

Compute

\displaystyle D_{r_{1}+1,r_{2},r_{3}}=D_{r_{1},r_{2},r_{3}}+2S^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}-2s_{m_{3}+r_{2}-r_{1}-2r_{3}-1}+C_{1}^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}.

23:

end

for

24:

Let

25:

if

r_{2}<m_{2}

then

26:

Let
r_{1}=0
. Denote
c_{2}=r_{2}+1-r_{1}-2r_{3}+m_{3}
, and compute

\displaystyle C_{2}^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}=m_{1}-n^{\prime}+\left|c_{2}-R\left(X(m_{2}-r),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)\right|

\displaystyle-\left|c_{2}+n-m_{3}-m_{2}-R\left(X(m_{2}-r),(X(l))_{l\in([n]\setminus W)\setminus U}\right)\right|.

27:

Compute

\displaystyle D_{r_{1},r_{2}+1,r_{3}}=D_{r_{1},r_{2},r_{3}}-2R^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}+2s_{m_{3}+r_{2}-r_{1}-2r_{3}}+C_{2}^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}.

28:

end

if

29:

end

for

30:

if

r_{3}<m_{3}

then

31:

Let
r_{1}=r_{2}=0
and compute

\displaystyle D_{r_{1},r_{2},r_{3}+1}

\displaystyle=D_{r_{1},r_{2},r_{3}}+|2r_{3}+1-n|-|n+1-2(m_{3}-r_{3})|

\displaystyle+2S^{(r_{1},r_{2},r_{3})}+2S^{(r_{1}+1,r_{2},r_{3})}-2m_{2}+2R^{(r_{1},r_{2}-1,r_{3})}+2R^{(r_{1},r_{2}-2,r_{3})}

\displaystyle-2m_{1}+2s_{2r_{3}+r_{1}-r_{2}-m_{3}}+2s_{2r_{3}+r_{1}-r_{2}-m_{3}+1}-2n^{\prime}.

32:

end

if

33:

end

for

34:

Return
\max\{D_{0,0,0},\ldots,D_{m_{1},m_{2},m_{3}}\}
.

Remark 9
.

A few comments of Algorithm
8
are made below:

In Algorithm
8
, the initialization
D_{0,0,0}
computed in
line
17
equals to
D(X_{*}^{(0,0)},Y_{*}^{(0,0)})
defined in
Proposition
E.15
.
Spearman’s footrule

D_{r_{1}+1,r_{2},r_{3}}
,
D_{r_{1},r_{2}+1,r_{3}}
,

D_{r_{1},r_{2},r_{3}+1}
,
and
D_{r_{1},r_{2},r_{3}}
equal to

D(X_{*}^{(r_{1}+1,r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})})
,

D(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2}+1,r_{3})})
,

D(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3}+1)},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3}+1)})
,
and
D(X_{*}^{(r_{1},r_{3})},Y_{*}^{(r_{2},r_{3})})
,
respectively, and are updated according to
Proposition
E.16
.

By computing
\{D_{0,0,0},\ldots,D_{m_{1},m_{2},m_{3}}\}
, Algorithm
8

finds all possible Spearman’s footrule
values
D\left(X_{*},Y_{*}\right)
, where
(X_{*},Y_{*})\in(\mathcal{X}(Y;U,[n]\setminus W,n),\mathcal{X}(X;V,[n]\setminus W,n))\cap\mathcal{Y}(W,n)
are imputations
X
and
Y
for indices
U\cup W
and
V\cup W
, respectively.
See discussions following Theorem 2.21 in the main paper
for explanations.
Hence, according to Theorem
C.18
,
the algorithm guarantees to find the maximum possible Spearman’s footrule
between
X
and
Y
.

The computational complexity of Algorithm
8
is analyzed as follows.
Ranking and relabeling all observed components in
X
and
Y
in line
5

requires
\mathcal{O}(n\log n)
steps.
Using these ranks, in line
7
computing each
d_{i}
is
\mathcal{O}(1)
,
and so overall line
7
is
\mathcal{O}(n-m_{1}-m_{2}-m_{3})
.
Line
8
and line
9
takes
\mathcal{O}(m_{2}^{2})

and
\mathcal{O}(m_{1})
steps, respectively.

According to Remark
5
, the computational
complexity of running Algorithm
4
in
line
10
and line
11
is
\mathcal{O}(m_{1}+m_{2}+m_{3})
,
and
\mathcal{O}(m_{2}+m_{3})
, respectively. Line
11
takes
\mathcal{O}(m_{2}m_{3})
steps.
In line
14
, each iteration of running Algorithm
4

within the for loop is
\mathcal{O}(m_{1}+m_{3})
.
Line
15
takes
\mathcal{O}(m_{1}m_{3})
.
Since the loop runs
m_{2}+1
times,
the computational complexity for the loop is
\mathcal{O}(m_{1}m_{2}m_{3})
.

Line
17
requires
\mathcal{O}(n)
steps using the ranks of observed components.
Line
21
, line
22
,
line
26
, and line
27

all take
\mathcal{O}(1)
steps. Since the loop in line
20

runs
m_{1}
times,
the computational complexity for the loop is
\mathcal{O}(m_{1})
.
Since line
31
takes
\mathcal{O}(1)
steps,
each iteration within the loop in line
19

takes
\mathcal{O}(m_{1}+1)
steps. Since the loop in line
19

runs
m_{2}
times,
the computational complexity for this loop is
\mathcal{O}(m_{1}m_{2})
.
Finally, the loop in line
18

runs
m_{3}
times. Hence,
the computational complexity for this
loop is
\mathcal{O}(m_{1}m_{2}m_{3})
.

Therefore, the overall computational complexity for Algorithm
6
is
\mathcal{O}(n\log n+m_{2}^{2}+m_{1}m_{2}m_{3})
.

n=100
.

Figure 18:
Statistical Type I error and power of imputation methods
as the proportion of missing data
s
increases,
where
s\in\{0.00,0.02,\ldots,0.30\}
.
The imputation methods impute missing data using either mean, median or
randomly selected (hot deck) values of observed data.
These methods are described in Table
6
.
The data is missing completely at random (MNAR).
(Left) Type I error:
(X,Y)\overset{iid}{\sim}N(0,I_{2})
;
(Right) Power:
(X,Y)\overset{iid}{\sim}N(0,\Sigma)
, where

\Sigma=\begin{pmatrix}1&\gamma\\
\gamma&1\end{pmatrix}
,
with covariance coefficient
\gamma=0.5
.
For both figures, a significance level
\alpha=0.05
is used and the sample
size
n=200
. The results in the figures are average of 1000 trials.

Note
: this figure is generated following the same approach for generating
Figure 5 in the main paper, with different correlation coefficient
\gamma=0.3
.

G.2.4
A different missing not at random missingness mechanism

Now we perform simulations following the same was as in Section 3.2
in the main paper when data are missing not at random (MNAR), with
a different MNAR missingness mechanism described below.

The way that the index set
T\subset\{1,\dots,n\}
of the missing components
is chosen depends on the values of
X
and
Y
.
Let
q=\sum_{i=1}^{n}I\left(\left|R\left(X(i),X\right)-R\left(Y(i),Y\right)\right|<n/2\right)

be the number of pairs of components in
X
and
Y
such that
\left|R\left(X(i),X\right)-R\left(Y(i),Y\right)\right|<n/2
.
Each index
i\in[n]

is selected to be in the set
T
of indices of missing components according to the
following probability conditional on
C=|T|
, the size of the set
T
:

\displaystyle p\left(i\in T\,|\,C=\lfloor s\cdot n\rfloor\right)=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\min\left\{1,sn/q\right\},&\mbox{if}\left|R\left(X(i),X\right)-R\left(Y(i),Y\right)\right|<n/2,\\
\max\{0,(sn-q)/(n-q)\},&\mbox{otherwise,}\end{array}\right.

for any given
s
. The results are shown in Figure
27
–
29
.

Figure 27:
Statistical Type I error and power of the proposed method,
methods that ignore missing data or use complete data,
and Alvo and Cabilio’s
\rho
and Alvo and Cabilio’s
\tau
methods,
as the proportion of missing data
s
increases,
where
s\in\{0.00,0.02,\ldots,0.30\}
.
These methods are described in Table
6
.
The data is missing completely at random (MCAR).
(Left) Type I error:
(X,Y)\overset{iid}{\sim}N(0,I_{2})
;
(Right) Power:
(X,Y)\overset{iid}{\sim}N(0,\Sigma)
, where

\Sigma=\begin{pmatrix}1&\gamma\\
\gamma&1\end{pmatrix}
,
with covariance coefficient
\gamma=0.5
.
For both figures, a significance level
\alpha=0.05
is used and the sample
size
n=200
. The results in the figures are average of 1000 trials.

Note
: this figure is generated following the same approach for generating
Figure 4 in the main paper, with a different missingness mechanism defined in (
G.2.4
).
